- **rename-spaces**: Rename a file by replacing all spaces (including Unicode variants like narrow no-break space U+202F) with underscores. The argument is a hint path — the directory must be exact, but spaces in the filename are matched fuzzily against any Unicode space character, so the user doesn't need to type the exact bytes. Only renames files, not directories. Use this skill whenever the user asks to rename a file that has spaces, or uses the /rename-spaces command, or mentions a file path where spaces are causing problems.
